## Outcome and authority

An environment-owned, bounded tick runs S3 `process-jobs`, conditionally runs S4 `match-findings` for selected tenant/Sites with a new matcher fence or version, then runs the existing S4-02c consequence drain. A late accepted reading reaches a revised FindingRevision and consequence without an operator invoking the two Leg B commands. Operators can see per-Site backlog, last success/failure, retry and stopped-worker conditions. S5-01 cannot mark a selected window CURRENT while relevant upstream work or an unobserved/stopped worker could leave it behind.

Authority: `.agent/architect-S4-02c-answers.md` R1, `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-14-s402d-schedule-storage-and-install-gate` (`.agent/architect-S4-02d-answers.md`). This packet owns registry node `I-analytic-finding-worker-schedule` (S). The separate F1 node owns run-1 sequence numbering and money limitation semantics; this packet does not change S3 cohort rules, Finding limits or price eligibility.

Built anchors: `.ai/CODE_STATE.md` S3-01/S4-01/S4-02c; `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assess_dispatch.py` (`process-jobs`, `match-findings --tenant-id --site-id`); `analytics/assessment_worker.py` (reconcile, leased jobs and retry); `backend/assetops_backend/recompute_consequences.py` and `financials/recompute.py` (Site claim, attempt-derived retry); `backend/README-db.md` (one-minute per-Site consequence-timer runbook); `tools/s402c_late_power_walkthrough.py` (Leg A/B proof). The current `process-jobs` CLI reconciles all assessed Sites and drains up to 1000 globally leased jobs; `match-findings` is a one-shot Site matcher; the consequence CLI serves only the configured development tenant and one named Site. Do not claim those CLIs already cover all active production Sites.

## Schedule, state and S5 contract

1. **Owned Site inventory and cadence.** One timer per environment invokes a server-side tick, initially every minute. An active Site is a persisted, tenant-scoped imported Site with registered evidence or explicit schedule enrolment, whose lifecycle is neither `DECOMMISSIONED` nor `ARCHIVED`; `PLANNED` qualifies (including KOBO-001). A Site with live S3 or pending consequence work remains selected until that work is resolved, even if its enrolment changes. Report excluded Sites that still have work; never silently discard it. Enumerate by stable IDs from the persisted Site registry on every tick; â€œnewly activatedâ€ in proof means newly imported/enrolled, not a lifecycle transition. Bound Sites, Site-scoped S3 leases, and consequence items per tick with a durable fair cursor. Extend the assessment store's currently global `lease_job` to filter by Site inside the existing assessment writer; its global FIFO cannot provide Site fairness. Run `reconcile_all()` each tick and reconcile every selected Site, including never-assessed Sites; measure its global unbounded cost, without silently omitting it. For each selected Site run S3, then run S4 only when there is no `finding_match_pass` for the current `MATCHER_VERSION`, or the Site's matcher fence (`_fence`: selection seq, job count/marker, revision count) differs from the fence stored in `schedule_site_head` at the last completed pass. Otherwise record `S4 skipped: unchanged fence` in that mutable head and append no pass row. Due, leased or FAILED S3 work does not by itself suppress a match when the fence moves. The matcher's CURRENT-window rule leaves those windows' episodes unchanged; unrelated current windows can advance after a new selection moves the fence. Drain consequence work after Finding commits. A bounded tick may leave work for later ticks.
2. **Concurrency and recovery.** Use database claims for the environment tick and Site stage sequence across hosts; local timer overlap prevention is insufficient. Never overlap S3 leasing/processing and S4 matching on the same Site, including a second host. The matcher fence includes Site recompute-job statuses; report `FindingFenceMoved` as retryable typed S4 `FENCE_MOVED`, not a generic success. Preserve S3's five-minute lease and retry and S4-02c's Site claim/append idempotency. An expired/crashed claim resumes without duplicate output. Bound tick and Site wall time with server-side `statement_timeout` and `lock_timeout`, plus a killable child process per stage for a Python hang. The Site S3 stage bound must be shorter than the five-minute lease; after a killed job, expose its lease expiry and retry, never assert immediate completion. Both DB stall and nonreturning-stage injections must yield typed `TIMEOUT` and retryable work. Give schedule-level discovery/stage failures retry/backoff without advancing success; do not add an independent retry to S3 or consequence rows. A terminal FAILED S3 job stays live and will not be re-enqueued by `reconcile`; expose its ID and `last_error`, alarm, and provide `assess_dispatch requeue --tenant-id --site-id --start --end` through the existing `AssessmentStore.enqueue` path for an operator to re-enqueue that default window after fixing the cause. Document the command and confirm the new DEFAULT selection supersedes the old failure; `assess` creates an EXPLICIT run and is not recovery.
3. **Durable state and health.** Migration `0013` adds `schedule_tick_head` (mutable environment owner, last tick start/success/failure, heartbeat, retry due and claim age), `schedule_site_head` (mutable per-Site claim/stage, selected/processed/deferred counts, last failure and last completed matcher fence), `schedule_cursor` (mutable fair position), and `schedule_enrolment` (mutable explicit Site enrolment). Use DB claims or equivalent expiring ownership in these records; do not add unbounded tick rows by default. The ratified eighth PostgreSQL writer, `backend/assetops_backend/scheduling/adapters/postgres_schedule_store.py`, is the sole runtime writer of these four tables. Amend `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1` rule 8 for this exact adapter path and these tables only: add writer allowlist and non-vacuity checks for an observed SQL write and driver import; allow `INSERT` and name the allowed mutable schedule-state `UPDATE` columns in the guard. Preserve reverse ownership: no other runtime module writes these tables, and this adapter writes no other product table. Test rejection of unlisted columns/tables. No wildcard SQL or driver exception, `DELETE`, `TRUNCATE`, runtime DDL, or weakening of the seven existing writers is allowed. Register a schedule domain composition in adapter-isolation checks if it imports this adapter; callers receive its port. Migration DDL is separate from runtime writing; any proposed trigger needs separate review. Claims and deadlines use DB time. Keep durable heads bounded; if attempt history is added, set a retention policy while preserving the latest failure and aggregate counts. Derive S3 backlog from live jobs, S4 backlog from existing `PostgresFindingStore.match_state` / `finding_match_pass` versus `analytic_run_selection.seq`, and consequence backlog from `ConsequenceStore.pending_work` with attempt count/age. Also count pre-0012 latest consequence heads whose read says `STALE` plus `NO_WORK_RECORDED` as distinct `STALE_NO_WORK` entries with Finding/revision IDs. This slice reports those discrepancies and blocks CURRENT; it does not backfill them. A read-only operator report gives each Site and aggregate `RUNNING`, `BACKLOG`, `RETRY_DUE`, `FAILED`, `STOPPED` or healthy idle. Only a completed zero-work tick advances heartbeat; claimed/skipped, failed and timed-out ticks do not. Repeated Site-claim skips, stale in-flight claims, old failures and growing uncapped consequence attempts trigger alarms and documented recovery.
   The rule 8 update scope is environment tick claim/lease, start/success/failure, heartbeat and retry due in `schedule_tick_head`; Site claim/lease, stage, selected/processed/deferred counts, last failure and completed matcher fence/skip state in `schedule_site_head`; fair position in `schedule_cursor`; and explicit enrolment state in `schedule_enrolment`. Name actual columns under those categories in the guard. S3 jobs and selections, Findings, accepted evidence, Foundation Sites, financial assumptions, and consequence work/revisions/attempts stay under their existing writers.
4. **S5-01 consumption.** Add a backend Python scoped read/port taking `(tenant_id, site_id, [window_start, window_end))`. Return the S3 `UNASSESSED|PENDING|CURRENT|STALE_PROJECTION|FAILED` read status and limits, accepted input/source basis, relevant live S3 job IDs/status/errors, selected `analytic_run_selection.seq` and run/revision IDs, `match_state` pass ID/matched-through seq and matched Finding revision IDs, consequence head/work IDs, status/attempts/age (including `STALE_NO_WORK`), schedule state/heartbeat, DB `clock_timestamp()`, and a derived upstream eligibility status with stage/reason. The accepted-source watermark is the selected S3 input basis plus its `analytic_run_selection.seq`; do not use disposition timestamps or a later heartbeat as a commit-order fence (`D-2026-10-11-s3-phase-2-selection-and-read`). Acceptance transactionally creates/widens a job. For each window and its six-hour continuity bracket, CURRENT eligibility requires no intersecting live S3 job, current S3 basis, `match_state.matched = true`, no relevant outstanding or stale-without-work consequence, and a healthy observed schedule; a stopped/unobserved schedule blocks it even with empty queues. Use DB time for heartbeat/STOPPED threshold and avoid app-clock assumptions. First build with work is `PENDING`; an old result with advanced basis/work is `STALE_PROJECTION`; failed with no usable prior result is `FAILED`, preserving typed stage/error. The existing S3 read status, matcher pass and pending-work derivations are authority; S5-01 consumes this seam and applies its separate F1 money rule and same-basis `CURRENT/PRICED` test. Historical IDs remain readable.

**Per-environment ownership and timer transition (phase 2).** Installation or enablement of a persistent schedule on any owner-visible host, including the demo host, is gated on `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` landing. Private development and isolated walkthrough ticks may run before then. On installing this tick, retire the S4-02c one-timer-per-Site consequence schedule in `backend/README-db.md`; keep its CLI for one-off operator recovery. A simultaneous legacy timer would hold the Site claim and produce false skip alarms. Document exact identity, credential, cadence, bounds, stop threshold and restart commands in that runbook.

| Environment | Tick owner and identity | Cadence / gate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Local development | Developer's local scheduler; local database credential | Optional one-minute tick on a private DB; remove the old per-Site timer when enabled. |
| Demo host | Deployment operator; dedicated schedule credential scoped to that database | One minute only after `I-late-reading-series-and-money-limit` lands; before then do not install. |
| Isolated walkthrough database | Walkthrough runner with isolated DB credential | Explicit tick invocations for repeatable proof; no persistent timer. |
| Real-Site pilot | Pilot deployment operator and separately provisioned schedule credential | Not installed until `real_pilot_requires` and other pilot gates close; then one environment timer at reviewed cadence. |

## Phases and focused proof

Each phase is a reviewable result fitting one Claude Implementer context below approximately 120k tokens; obtain an independent fresh review before proceeding.

| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
| --- | --- |
| 1. Bounded orchestration and storage | Migration 0013, new schedule writer and reviewed rule 8 amendment; persisted active-Site enumeration, fair bounded tick, Site-scoped S3 leasing, ordered S3 â†’ conditional S4 â†’ consequence invocation, cross-host claims and timeout/restart behavior. Prove two tenants, newly imported/enrolled `PLANNED` Site, excluded/foreign Site reporting, more Sites/jobs than one tick limit, two concurrent ticks, crash/restart and idempotent replay. Inject DB stall and a nonreturning stage. Prove N idle ticks append zero `finding_match_pass` rows and a new selection appends exactly one pass, while retaining the per-window currentness property: S4 runs but does not change episodes over a due/leased/FAILED S3 window, that window cannot be CURRENT, and another current window's Finding still advances. |
| 2. +# S4-05 — stored demonstration provenance
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the existing Finding/Financials “Demonstration data” and “Source not confirmed” labels change source; the owner must approve mixed/unknown wording and pilot designation at the W1 checkpoint. Backend-only implementation, with no new screen, may be reviewed independently but its owner-visible label change holds merge until that checkpoint.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials / S2 Evidence follow-on; size M, two phases
+Branch: `plan/stored-demo-provenance` for this packet; implement on a fresh `task/S4-05-stored-demo-provenance` branch from reviewed, merged main. Coordinator commits this packet.
+Depends on registry `A-S2-evidence`, `A-actor-identity`, `I-S2-02`, `I-finding-read`, `I-financial-read` (resolved on main). This packet does not depend on unmerged LR-01, S5-01 or S6-01.
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-15
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+The server records who authorized an exact demonstration publication before gateway upload, pins its tenant/Site, immutable publication ID and fixture/spec version, and binds its accepted evidence IDs. A selected FindingRevision and every consequence revision show provenance from their **actual cited evidence**, including mixed and unknown cases. A real KOBO-001 Finding on the same day as an authored fixture is never labelled demonstration by date. This closes `I-stored-demo-provenance` only after review, owner copy approval, and merge; it is required before W1 exit and any real-Site pilot, alongside the other `real_pilot_requires` gates.
+
+Authority: registry `I-stored-demo-provenance` acceptance target (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:43`); `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md:17-21` Q3; `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md:12` work order item 4; actor decision `.ai/DECISIONS.md:3987-3989`; backlog `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2157,2192-2199`. `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41,56` already withholds an amount for an unverified demonstration source; this packet must preserve or strengthen that gate when S5-01 later consumes the source state.
+
+## Code reality on merged main
+
+- `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:116-145,161-210` has tenant/Site-scoped `ingestion_batch`, `accepted_evidence`, `batch_id` and composite foreign keys, but no publication author or fixture version. `source_mode` is `LIVE|SIMULATED`, not proof of authorship. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:368-413` defines the ledger contract.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:264-275,287-345` authenticates a registered gateway for V2 `POST /api/ingestion/batches`; this principal only permits upload. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_evidence_ledger.py:202-240,242-307,309-327` opens a digest-pinned batch and atomically persists accepted evidence/dispositions in chunks. It is the **existing writer to extend**; no ninth writer is proposed.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-204,308-330` stores exact `finding_revision_evidence` IDs and reads their lineage. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_read.py:350-438,446-550` exposes selected revision and Evidence reads. `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:87-132,149-221` exposes consequence versions with power-interval evidence IDs and current amount status. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:247-277,284-308` serves the consequence and Financials routes.
+- The retiring heuristic is `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:36-45,60-77`: it loads an on-disk case spec and labels solely by Site code and UTC day, or “Source not confirmed” when unreadable. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275-276,306` calls it on both money reads. `backend/tests/test_s4_assumption_read_api.py:269-307` asserts this old behavior; replace those assertions with stored-provenance cases.
+- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:621-625,678-704,828-840` rule 8 owns the ledger table allowlist and rejects other runtime database writers/driver imports. `backend/assetops_backend/publish_assumption.py:1-4,104-164` is an existing SYSTEM-only *pattern*, not a provenance publisher. `backend/TESTING.md:7-29` requires focused phase tests and one final-revision real-Postgres suite.
+
+## Implementation contract
+
+1. **Publication, before upload (new).** Add migration `0014` from main's `0013_schedule_state` head for an append-only `evidence_publication` table and a scoped immutable batch-publication binding (either a nullable `ingestion_batch.publication_id` or a one-to-one insert-only link). Publication has UUID ID; tenant/Site UUID; external batch ID, canonical Batch V2 payload SHA-256, gateway/source IDs; designation `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION`; fixture/spec ID and version; server-resolved `actor_kind=SYSTEM`, stable service principal, authorization basis, source channel, triggering job/fixture ID, and server `recorded_at`. Use composite tenant/Site FKs, unique publication identity and unique exact batch commitment; prohibit update/delete or conflicting reuse. A real/live assertion is **not** inferred from absence of designation or `source_mode=LIVE`.
+2. **Authorization (new).** Expose only a server-side administrative SYSTEM fixture/CLI command with fixed service principal and explicit configured allowlist/authorization basis, modelled on `publish_assumption.py`; no public gateway or browser write route. It must validate fixture/spec identity/version and canonical batch digest against the exact payload, then insert the publication through an extension of `PostgresEvidenceLedger`. No `--actor`, `--author` or client-supplied provenance text can become authority. Refuse wrong tenant/Site, gateway/source, missing or conflicting batch digest, publication ID reuse with changed content, and publication after any batch admission. An identical retry returns the same publication. Registration and subsequent `open_batch` must serialize for the same scoped batch key so ingestion cannot race an after-the-fact designation. The upload remains valid without designation but has `UNKNOWN` authorship.
+3. **Accepted binding (new).** `open_batch` matches only the preauthorized exact tenant/Site/gateway/source/external-batch-ID/digest commitment. The accepted evidence already references the scoped batch (`0005:165,197-200`); expose the immutable publication ID per **accepted evidence ID** through that link, including interrupted/resumed chunks. A duplicate disposition retains the original accepted ID and its original provenance; it cannot relabel old evidence. Rejected/conflict rows confer no provenance. No retroactive assignment or mutable backfill of historic accepted rows. Fail the upload if a matching publication conflicts; never silently downgrade a claimed publication to unknown.
+4. **Read and amount rules (new).** Resolve every cited accepted ID under the requested tenant/Site, returning publication ID, designation and fixture/spec version per record plus a set-level state: `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only when every relevant cited ID is bound to a consistent authored publication basis; `MIXED` when known designations differ; `UNKNOWN` for any missing/unresolved/mismatched link (and retain exact problematic IDs). A FindingRevision uses its immutable cited set, not an episode date. A consequence uses its **own** quantified power-interval evidence IDs as well as the selected FindingRevision cited set; a later revision can differ. Preserve historical revision-specific results; do not relabel a historical revision from the current head. Mixed/unknown reads say “Source not confirmed” with exact IDs and show no current amount wherever this source gate is used. Preserve `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41` no-amount rule when its branch merges. Keep existing “Demonstration data” wording for fully authored P01; propose concise mixed/unknown copy to owner at checkpoint. Remove runtime calls to `demonstration_case` and its date/spec cache; retain its old test only as regression proving same-day real/unknown is not labelled authored.
+5. **Writer and migration boundary.** Extend the existing ledger writer's allowed tables in rule 8 (`configuration-persistence.ps1:624-625`); require INSERT only for publication/link rows, and prevent ledger or other writers from UPDATE/DELETE of these immutable rows. Preserve all eight existing writer allowlists, reverse ownership and driver non-vacuity. Add guard probes for permitted ledger INSERT, forbidden other-writer INSERT, mutation, omitted writer/import, and client route attempting designation. If LR-01 merges first and contributes migrations 0014/0015 on its branch, re-chain this migration to the new head and renumber it; never create a parallel head. Recheck S5-01 read composition after its merge without stacking this task on that unmerged branch.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Stored authorization and binding | Migration, SYSTEM publication command, ledger extension, rule-8 amendment. New focused Postgres tests: two tenants/same Site code; wrong Site, gateway/source, payload digest and post-admission refusal; client-only and gateway-credential authorship refusal; identical retry versus conflicting reuse; registration/upload race; partial batch restart; accepted/duplicate/rejected/conflict IDs; immutable SQL mutation refusal; guard probes. Review phase 1 independently. |
+| 2. Finding and consequence lineage | New scoped resolver and read DTOs; retire date heuristic in both `finding_api.py` money routes; use selected FindingRevision and consequence evidence IDs. New focused API/Postgres tests: authored P01, ordinary real/unknown reading on P01's exact day, mixed cited sets, missing publication/link, wrong-Site cited ID, historical revision, changed consequence evidence set and no-current-amount for mixed/unknown. Run adjacent Finding/Evidence/Financials tests and `tools/check-all.ps1`. Independent phase-2 review is the slice review. |
+
+At slice end run **one** final-revision real-Postgres full suite per `backend/TESTING.md`: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml`, with `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` on a `*_test` database, zero failed/skipped JUnit cases, the keep-awake helper and 2× documented runtime cap. Run `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs`. Record focused/full commands, IDs, old/new API JSON, and an exact P01 plus same-day ordinary source walkthrough in the review handoff. Obtain owner wording/pilot-designation acceptance before merge because existing visible labels change. Do not claim W1 exit or pilot readiness from this task alone.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. **Which publication moment is authoritative?** Recommend pre-admission, digest-pinned SYSTEM registration with a serialized batch-key lock (contract above); it blocks retroactive authorship and leaves gateway transport unchanged. Architect ratifies before phase 1 if another moment is required.
+2. **Can several authored publications contribute one Finding?** Recommend `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only if all cited IDs have the same fixture/spec version and publication basis, with exact publication IDs listed; otherwise `MIXED`/`UNKNOWN`. Architect confirms the set rule before phase 2.
+3. **How should unknown source gate money?** Recommend the S5-01 cautious no-amount behavior for all current money reads, including Finding Financials and consequence, with audit-only historical values still linked. Architect confirms precedence against existing `CURRENT PRICED` and LR eligibility before phase 2.

+

+# S4-05 — stored demonstration provenance
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the existing Finding/Financials “Demonstration data” and “Source not confirmed” labels change source; the owner must approve mixed/unknown wording and pilot designation at the W1 checkpoint. Backend-only implementation, with no new screen, may be reviewed independently but its owner-visible label change holds merge until that checkpoint.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials / S2 Evidence follow-on; size M, two phases
+Branch: `plan/stored-demo-provenance` for this packet; implement on a fresh `task/S4-05-stored-demo-provenance` branch from reviewed, merged main. Coordinator commits this packet.
+Depends on registry `A-S2-evidence`, `A-actor-identity`, `I-S2-02`, `I-finding-read`, `I-financial-read` (resolved on main). This packet does not depend on unmerged LR-01, S5-01 or S6-01.
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-15
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+The server records who authorized an exact demonstration publication before gateway upload, pins its tenant/Site, immutable publication ID and fixture/spec version, and binds its accepted evidence IDs. A selected FindingRevision and every consequence revision show provenance from their **actual cited evidence**, including mixed and unknown cases. A real KOBO-001 Finding on the same day as an authored fixture is never labelled demonstration by date. This closes `I-stored-demo-provenance` only after review, owner copy approval, and merge; it is required before W1 exit and any real-Site pilot, alongside the other `real_pilot_requires` gates.
+
+Authority: registry `I-stored-demo-provenance` acceptance target (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:43`); `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md:17-21` Q3; `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md:12` work order item 4; actor decision `.ai/DECISIONS.md:3987-3989`; backlog `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2157,2192-2199`. `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41,56` already withholds an amount for an unverified demonstration source; this packet must preserve or strengthen that gate when S5-01 later consumes the source state.
+
+## Code reality on merged main
+
+- `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:116-145,161-210` has tenant/Site-scoped `ingestion_batch`, `accepted_evidence`, `batch_id` and composite foreign keys, but no publication author or fixture version. `source_mode` is `LIVE|SIMULATED`, not proof of authorship. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:368-413` defines the ledger contract.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:264-275,287-345` authenticates a registered gateway for V2 `POST /api/ingestion/batches`; this principal only permits upload. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_evidence_ledger.py:202-240,242-307,309-327` opens a digest-pinned batch and atomically persists accepted evidence/dispositions in chunks. It is the **existing writer to extend**; no ninth writer is proposed.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-204,308-330` stores exact `finding_revision_evidence` IDs and reads their lineage. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_read.py:350-438,446-550` exposes selected revision and Evidence reads. `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:87-132,149-221` exposes consequence versions with power-interval evidence IDs and current amount status. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:247-277,284-308` serves the consequence and Financials routes.
+- The retiring heuristic is `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:36-45,60-77`: it loads an on-disk case spec and labels solely by Site code and UTC day, or “Source not confirmed” when unreadable. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275-276,306` calls it on both money reads. `backend/tests/test_s4_assumption_read_api.py:269-307` asserts this old behavior; replace those assertions with stored-provenance cases.
+- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:621-625,678-704,828-840` rule 8 owns the ledger table allowlist and rejects other runtime database writers/driver imports. `backend/assetops_backend/publish_assumption.py:1-4,104-164` is an existing SYSTEM-only *pattern*, not a provenance publisher. `backend/TESTING.md:7-29` requires focused phase tests and one final-revision real-Postgres suite.
+
+## Implementation contract
+
+1. **Publication, before upload (new).** Add migration `0014` from main's `0013_schedule_state` head for an append-only `evidence_publication` table and a scoped immutable batch-publication binding (either a nullable `ingestion_batch.publication_id` or a one-to-one insert-only link). Publication has UUID ID; tenant/Site UUID; external batch ID, canonical Batch V2 payload SHA-256, gateway/source IDs; designation `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION`; fixture/spec ID and version; server-resolved `actor_kind=SYSTEM`, stable service principal, authorization basis, source channel, triggering job/fixture ID, and server `recorded_at`. Use composite tenant/Site FKs, unique publication identity and unique exact batch commitment; prohibit update/delete or conflicting reuse. A real/live assertion is **not** inferred from absence of designation or `source_mode=LIVE`.
+2. **Authorization (new).** Expose only a server-side administrative SYSTEM fixture/CLI command with fixed service principal and explicit configured allowlist/authorization basis, modelled on `publish_assumption.py`; no public gateway or browser write route. It must validate fixture/spec identity/version and canonical batch digest against the exact payload, then insert the publication through an extension of `PostgresEvidenceLedger`. No `--actor`, `--author` or client-supplied provenance text can become authority. Refuse wrong tenant/Site, gateway/source, missing or conflicting batch digest, publication ID reuse with changed content, and publication after any batch admission. An identical retry returns the same publication. Registration and subsequent `open_batch` must serialize for the same scoped batch key so ingestion cannot race an after-the-fact designation. The upload remains valid without designation but has `UNKNOWN` authorship.
+3. **Accepted binding (new).** `open_batch` matches only the preauthorized exact tenant/Site/gateway/source/external-batch-ID/digest commitment. The accepted evidence already references the scoped batch (`0005:165,197-200`); expose the immutable publication ID per **accepted evidence ID** through that link, including interrupted/resumed chunks. A duplicate disposition retains the original accepted ID and its original provenance; it cannot relabel old evidence. Rejected/conflict rows confer no provenance. No retroactive assignment or mutable backfill of historic accepted rows. Fail the upload if a matching publication conflicts; never silently downgrade a claimed publication to unknown.
+4. **Read and amount rules (new).** Resolve every cited accepted ID under the requested tenant/Site, returning publication ID, designation and fixture/spec version per record plus a set-level state: `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only when every relevant cited ID is bound to a consistent authored publication basis; `MIXED` when known designations differ; `UNKNOWN` for any missing/unresolved/mismatched link (and retain exact problematic IDs). A FindingRevision uses its immutable cited set, not an episode date. A consequence uses its **own** quantified power-interval evidence IDs as well as the selected FindingRevision cited set; a later revision can differ. Preserve historical revision-specific results; do not relabel a historical revision from the current head. Mixed/unknown reads say “Source not confirmed” with exact IDs and show no current amount wherever this source gate is used. Preserve `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41` no-amount rule when its branch merges. Keep existing “Demonstration data” wording for fully authored P01; propose concise mixed/unknown copy to owner at checkpoint. Remove runtime calls to `demonstration_case` and its date/spec cache; retain its old test only as regression proving same-day real/unknown is not labelled authored.
+5. **Writer and migration boundary.** Extend the existing ledger writer's allowed tables in rule 8 (`configuration-persistence.ps1:624-625`); require INSERT only for publication/link rows, and prevent ledger or other writers from UPDATE/DELETE of these immutable rows. Preserve all eight existing writer allowlists, reverse ownership and driver non-vacuity. Add guard probes for permitted ledger INSERT, forbidden other-writer INSERT, mutation, omitted writer/import, and client route attempting designation. If LR-01 merges first and contributes migrations 0014/0015 on its branch, re-chain this migration to the new head and renumber it; never create a parallel head. Recheck S5-01 read composition after its merge without stacking this task on that unmerged branch.
+

Review phase 1 independently. |

+# S4-05 — stored demonstration provenance
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the existing Finding/Financials “Demonstration data” and “Source not confirmed” labels change source; the owner must approve mixed/unknown wording and pilot designation at the W1 checkpoint. Backend-only implementation, with no new screen, may be reviewed independently but its owner-visible label change holds merge until that checkpoint.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials / S2 Evidence follow-on; size M, two phases
+Branch: `plan/stored-demo-provenance` for this packet; implement on a fresh `task/S4-05-stored-demo-provenance` branch from reviewed, merged main. Coordinator commits this packet.
+Depends on registry `A-S2-evidence`, `A-actor-identity`, `I-S2-02`, `I-finding-read`, `I-financial-read` (resolved on main). This packet does not depend on unmerged LR-01, S5-01 or S6-01.
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-15
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+The server records who authorized an exact demonstration publication before gateway upload, pins its tenant/Site, immutable publication ID and fixture/spec version, and binds its accepted evidence IDs. A selected FindingRevision and every consequence revision show provenance from their **actual cited evidence**, including mixed and unknown cases. A real KOBO-001 Finding on the same day as an authored fixture is never labelled demonstration by date. This closes `I-stored-demo-provenance` only after review, owner copy approval, and merge; it is required before W1 exit and any real-Site pilot, alongside the other `real_pilot_requires` gates.
+
+Authority: registry `I-stored-demo-provenance` acceptance target (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:43`); `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md:17-21` Q3; `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md:12` work order item 4; actor decision `.ai/DECISIONS.md:3987-3989`; backlog `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2157,2192-2199`. `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41,56` already withholds an amount for an unverified demonstration source; this packet must preserve or strengthen that gate when S5-01 later consumes the source state.
+
+## Code reality on merged main
+
+- `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:116-145,161-210` has tenant/Site-scoped `ingestion_batch`, `accepted_evidence`, `batch_id` and composite foreign keys, but no publication author or fixture version. `source_mode` is `LIVE|SIMULATED`, not proof of authorship. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:368-413` defines the ledger contract.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:264-275,287-345` authenticates a registered gateway for V2 `POST /api/ingestion/batches`; this principal only permits upload. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_evidence_ledger.py:202-240,242-307,309-327` opens a digest-pinned batch and atomically persists accepted evidence/dispositions in chunks. It is the **existing writer to extend**; no ninth writer is proposed.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-204,308-330` stores exact `finding_revision_evidence` IDs and reads their lineage. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_read.py:350-438,446-550` exposes selected revision and Evidence reads. `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:87-132,149-221` exposes consequence versions with power-interval evidence IDs and current amount status. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:247-277,284-308` serves the consequence and Financials routes.
+- The retiring heuristic is `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:36-45,60-77`: it loads an on-disk case spec and labels solely by Site code and UTC day, or “Source not confirmed” when unreadable. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275-276,306` calls it on both money reads. `backend/tests/test_s4_assumption_read_api.py:269-307` asserts this old behavior; replace those assertions with stored-provenance cases.
+- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:621-625,678-704,828-840` rule 8 owns the ledger table allowlist and rejects other runtime database writers/driver imports. `backend/assetops_backend/publish_assumption.py:1-4,104-164` is an existing SYSTEM-only *pattern*, not a provenance publisher. `backend/TESTING.md:7-29` requires focused phase tests and one final-revision real-Postgres suite.
+
+## Implementation contract
+
+1. **Publication, before upload (new).** Add migration `0014` from main's `0013_schedule_state` head for an append-only `evidence_publication` table and a scoped immutable batch-publication binding (either a nullable `ingestion_batch.publication_id` or a one-to-one insert-only link). Publication has UUID ID; tenant/Site UUID; external batch ID, canonical Batch V2 payload SHA-256, gateway/source IDs; designation `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION`; fixture/spec ID and version; server-resolved `actor_kind=SYSTEM`, stable service principal, authorization basis, source channel, triggering job/fixture ID, and server `recorded_at`. Use composite tenant/Site FKs, unique publication identity and unique exact batch commitment; prohibit update/delete or conflicting reuse. A real/live assertion is **not** inferred from absence of designation or `source_mode=LIVE`.
+2. **Authorization (new).** Expose only a server-side administrative SYSTEM fixture/CLI command with fixed service principal and explicit configured allowlist/authorization basis, modelled on `publish_assumption.py`; no public gateway or browser write route. It must validate fixture/spec identity/version and canonical batch digest against the exact payload, then insert the publication through an extension of `PostgresEvidenceLedger`. No `--actor`, `--author` or client-supplied provenance text can become authority. Refuse wrong tenant/Site, gateway/source, missing or conflicting batch digest, publication ID reuse with changed content, and publication after any batch admission. An identical retry returns the same publication. Registration and subsequent `open_batch` must serialize for the same scoped batch key so ingestion cannot race an after-the-fact designation. The upload remains valid without designation but has `UNKNOWN` authorship.
+3. **Accepted binding (new).** `open_batch` matches only the preauthorized exact tenant/Site/gateway/source/external-batch-ID/digest commitment. The accepted evidence already references the scoped batch (`0005:165,197-200`); expose the immutable publication ID per **accepted evidence ID** through that link, including interrupted/resumed chunks. A duplicate disposition retains the original accepted ID and its original provenance; it cannot relabel old evidence. Rejected/conflict rows confer no provenance. No retroactive assignment or mutable backfill of historic accepted rows. Fail the upload if a matching publication conflicts; never silently downgrade a claimed publication to unknown.
+4. **Read and amount rules (new).** Resolve every cited accepted ID under the requested tenant/Site, returning publication ID, designation and fixture/spec version per record plus a set-level state: `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only when every relevant cited ID is bound to a consistent authored publication basis; `MIXED` when known designations differ; `UNKNOWN` for any missing/unresolved/mismatched link (and retain exact problematic IDs). A FindingRevision uses its immutable cited set, not an episode date. A consequence uses its **own** quantified power-interval evidence IDs as well as the selected FindingRevision cited set; a later revision can differ. Preserve historical revision-specific results; do not relabel a historical revision from the current head. Mixed/unknown reads say “Source not confirmed” with exact IDs and show no current amount wherever this source gate is used. Preserve `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41` no-amount rule when its branch merges. Keep existing “Demonstration data” wording for fully authored P01; propose concise mixed/unknown copy to owner at checkpoint. Remove runtime calls to `demonstration_case` and its date/spec cache; retain its old test only as regression proving same-day real/unknown is not labelled authored.
+5. **Writer and migration boundary.** Extend the existing ledger writer's allowed tables in rule 8 (`configuration-persistence.ps1:624-625`); require INSERT only for publication/link rows, and prevent ledger or other writers from UPDATE/DELETE of these immutable rows. Preserve all eight existing writer allowlists, reverse ownership and driver non-vacuity. Add guard probes for permitted ledger INSERT, forbidden other-writer INSERT, mutation, omitted writer/import, and client route attempting designation. If LR-01 merges first and contributes migrations 0014/0015 on its branch, re-chain this migration to the new head and renumber it; never create a parallel head. Recheck S5-01 read composition after its merge without stacking this task on that unmerged branch.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Stored authorization and binding | Migration, SYSTEM publication command, ledger extension, rule-8 amendment. New focused Postgres tests: two tenants/same Site code; wrong Site, gateway/source, payload digest and post-admission refusal; client-only and gateway-credential authorship refusal; identical retry versus conflicting reuse; registration/upload race; partial batch restart; accepted/duplicate/rejected/conflict IDs; immutable SQL mutation refusal; guard probes. Review phase 1 independently. |
+| 2. Finding and consequence lineage | New scoped resolver and read DTOs; retire date heuristic in both `finding_api.py` money routes; use selected FindingRevision and consequence evidence IDs. New focused API/Postgres tests: authored P01, ordinary real/unknown reading on P01's exact day, mixed cited sets, missing publication/link, wrong-Site cited ID, historical revision, changed consequence evidence set and no-current-amount for mixed/unknown. Run adjacent Finding/Evidence/Financials tests and `tools/check-all.ps1`. Independent phase-2 review is the slice review. |
+
+At slice end run **one** final-revision real-Postgres full suite per `backend/TESTING.md`: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml`, with `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` on a `*_test` database, zero failed/skipped JUnit cases, the keep-awake helper and 2× documented runtime cap. Run `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs`. Record focused/full commands, IDs, old/new API JSON, and an exact P01 plus same-day ordinary source walkthrough in the review handoff. Obtain owner wording/pilot-designation acceptance before merge because existing visible labels change. Do not claim W1 exit or pilot readiness from this task alone.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. **Which publication moment is authoritative?** Recommend pre-admission, digest-pinned SYSTEM registration with a serialized batch-key lock (contract above); it blocks retroactive authorship and leaves gateway transport unchanged. Architect ratifies before phase 1 if another moment is required.
+2. **Can several authored publications contribute one Finding?** Recommend `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only if all cited IDs have the same fixture/spec version and publication basis, with exact publication IDs listed; otherwise `MIXED`/`UNKNOWN`. Architect confirms the set rule before phase 2.
+3. **How should unknown source gate money?** Recommend the S5-01 cautious no-amount behavior for all current money reads, including Finding Financials and consequence, with audit-only historical values still linked. Architect confirms precedence against existing `CURRENT PRICED` and LR eligibility before phase 2.

+# S4-05 — stored demonstration provenance
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the existing Finding/Financials “Demonstration data” and “Source not confirmed” labels change source; the owner must approve mixed/unknown wording and pilot designation at the W1 checkpoint. Backend-only implementation, with no new screen, may be reviewed independently but its owner-visible label change holds merge until that checkpoint.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials / S2 Evidence follow-on; size M, two phases
+Branch: `plan/stored-demo-provenance` for this packet; implement on a fresh `task/S4-05-stored-demo-provenance` branch from reviewed, merged main. Coordinator commits this packet.
+Depends on registry `A-S2-evidence`, `A-actor-identity`, `I-S2-02`, `I-finding-read`, `I-financial-read` (resolved on main). This packet does not depend on unmerged LR-01, S5-01 or S6-01.
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-15
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+The server records who authorized an exact demonstration publication before gateway upload, pins its tenant/Site, immutable publication ID and fixture/spec version, and binds its accepted evidence IDs. A selected FindingRevision and every consequence revision show provenance from their **actual cited evidence**, including mixed and unknown cases. A real KOBO-001 Finding on the same day as an authored fixture is never labelled demonstration by date. This closes `I-stored-demo-provenance` only after review, owner copy approval, and merge; it is required before W1 exit and any real-Site pilot, alongside the other `real_pilot_requires` gates.
+
+Authority: registry `I-stored-demo-provenance` acceptance target (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:43`); `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md:17-21` Q3; `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md:12` work order item 4; actor decision `.ai/DECISIONS.md:3987-3989`; backlog `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2157,2192-2199`. `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41,56` already withholds an amount for an unverified demonstration source; this packet must preserve or strengthen that gate when S5-01 later consumes the source state.
+
+## Code reality on merged main
+
+- `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:116-145,161-210` has tenant/Site-scoped `ingestion_batch`, `accepted_evidence`, `batch_id` and composite foreign keys, but no publication author or fixture version. `source_mode` is `LIVE|SIMULATED`, not proof of authorship. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:368-413` defines the ledger contract.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:264-275,287-345` authenticates a registered gateway for V2 `POST /api/ingestion/batches`; this principal only permits upload. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_evidence_ledger.py:202-240,242-307,309-327` opens a digest-pinned batch and atomically persists accepted evidence/dispositions in chunks. It is the **existing writer to extend**; no ninth writer is proposed.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-204,308-330` stores exact `finding_revision_evidence` IDs and reads their lineage. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_read.py:350-438,446-550` exposes selected revision and Evidence reads. `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:87-132,149-221` exposes consequence versions with power-interval evidence IDs and current amount status. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:247-277,284-308` serves the consequence and Financials routes.
+- The retiring heuristic is `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:36-45,60-77`: it loads an on-disk case spec and labels solely by Site code and UTC day, or “Source not confirmed” when unreadable. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275-276,306` calls it on both money reads. `backend/tests/test_s4_assumption_read_api.py:269-307` asserts this old behavior; replace those assertions with stored-provenance cases.
+- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:621-625,678-704,828-840` rule 8 owns the ledger table allowlist and rejects other runtime database writers/driver imports. `backend/assetops_backend/publish_assumption.py:1-4,104-164` is an existing SYSTEM-only *pattern*, not a provenance publisher. `backend/TESTING.md:7-29` requires focused phase tests and one final-revision real-Postgres suite.
+
+## Implementation contract
+
+1. **Publication, before upload (new).** Add migration `0014` from main's `0013_schedule_state` head for an append-only `evidence_publication` table and a scoped immutable batch-publication binding (either a nullable `ingestion_batch.publication_id` or a one-to-one insert-only link). Publication has UUID ID; tenant/Site UUID; external batch ID, canonical Batch V2 payload SHA-256, gateway/source IDs; designation `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION`; fixture/spec ID and version; server-resolved `actor_kind=SYSTEM`, stable service principal, authorization basis, source channel, triggering job/fixture ID, and server `recorded_at`. Use composite tenant/Site FKs, unique publication identity and unique exact batch commitment; prohibit update/delete or conflicting reuse. A real/live assertion is **not** inferred from absence of designation or `source_mode=LIVE`.
+2. **Authorization (new).** Expose only a server-side administrative SYSTEM fixture/CLI command with fixed service principal and explicit configured allowlist/authorization basis, modelled on `publish_assumption.py`; no public gateway or browser write route. It must validate fixture/spec identity/version and canonical batch digest against the exact payload, then insert the publication through an extension of `PostgresEvidenceLedger`. No `--actor`, `--author` or client-supplied provenance text can become authority. Refuse wrong tenant/Site, gateway/source, missing or conflicting batch digest, publication ID reuse with changed content, and publication after any batch admission. An identical retry returns the same publication. Registration and subsequent `open_batch` must serialize for the same scoped batch key so ingestion cannot race an after-the-fact designation. The upload remains valid without designation but has `UNKNOWN` authorship.
+3. **Accepted binding (new).** `open_batch` matches only the preauthorized exact tenant/Site/gateway/source/external-batch-ID/digest commitment. The accepted evidence already references the scoped batch (`0005:165,197-200`); expose the immutable publication ID per **accepted evidence ID** through that link, including interrupted/resumed chunks. A duplicate disposition retains the original accepted ID and its original provenance; it cannot relabel old evidence. Rejected/conflict rows confer no provenance. No retroactive assignment or mutable backfill of historic accepted rows. Fail the upload if a matching publication conflicts; never silently downgrade a claimed publication to unknown.
+4. **Read and amount rules (new).** Resolve every cited accepted ID under the requested tenant/Site, returning publication ID, designation and fixture/spec version per record plus a set-level state: `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only when every relevant cited ID is bound to a consistent authored publication basis; `MIXED` when known designations differ; `UNKNOWN` for any missing/unresolved/mismatched link (and retain exact problematic IDs). A FindingRevision uses its immutable cited set, not an episode date. A consequence uses its **own** quantified power-interval evidence IDs as well as the selected FindingRevision cited set; a later revision can differ. Preserve historical revision-specific results; do not relabel a historical revision from the current head. Mixed/unknown reads say “Source not confirmed” with exact IDs and show no current amount wherever this source gate is used. Preserve `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41` no-amount rule when its branch merges. Keep existing “Demonstration data” wording for fully authored P01; propose concise mixed/unknown copy to owner at checkpoint. Remove runtime calls to `demonstration_case` and its date/spec cache; retain its old test only as regression proving same-day real/unknown is not labelled authored.
+5. **Writer and migration boundary.** Extend the existing ledger writer's allowed tables in rule 8 (`configuration-persistence.ps1:624-625`); require INSERT only for publication/link rows, and prevent ledger or other writers from UPDATE/DELETE of these immutable rows. Preserve all eight existing writer allowlists, reverse ownership and driver non-vacuity. Add guard probes for permitted ledger INSERT, forbidden other-writer INSERT, mutation, omitted writer/import, and client route attempting designation. If LR-01 merges first and contributes migrations 0014/0015 on its branch, re-chain this migration to the new head and renumber it; never create a parallel head. Recheck S5-01 read composition after its merge without stacking this task on that unmerged branch.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Stored authorization and binding | Migration, SYSTEM publication command, ledger extension, rule-8 amendment. New focused Postgres tests: two tenants/same Site code; wrong Site, gateway/source, payload digest and post-admission refusal; client-only and gateway-credential authorship refusal; identical retry versus conflicting reuse; registration/upload race; partial batch restart; accepted/duplicate/rejected/conflict IDs; immutable SQL mutation refusal; guard probes. Review phase 1 independently. |
+| 2. Finding and consequence lineage | New scoped resolver and read DTOs; retire date heuristic in both `finding_api.py` money routes; use selected FindingRevision and consequence evidence IDs. New focused API/Postgres tests: authored P01, ordinary real/unknown reading on P01's exact day, mixed cited sets, missing publication/link, wrong-Site cited ID, historical revision, changed consequence evidence set and no-current-amount for mixed/unknown. Run adjacent Finding/Evidence/Financials tests and `tools/check-all.ps1`. Independent phase-2 review is the slice review. |
+
+At slice end run **one** final-revision real-Postgres full suite per `backend/TESTING.md`: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml`, with `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` on a `*_test` database, zero failed/skipped JUnit cases, the keep-awake helper and 2× documented runtime cap. Run `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs`. Record focused/full commands, IDs, old/new API JSON, and an exact P01 plus same-day ordinary source walkthrough in the review handoff. Obtain owner wording/pilot-designation acceptance before merge because existing visible labels change. Do not claim W1 exit or pilot readiness from this task alone.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. **Which publication moment is authoritative?** Recommend pre-admission, digest-pinned SYSTEM registration with a serialized batch-key lock (contract above); it blocks retroactive authorship and leaves gateway transport unchanged. Architect ratifies before phase 1 if another moment is required.
+2. **Can several authored publications contribute one Finding?** Recommend `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only if all cited IDs have the same fixture/spec version and publication basis, with exact publication IDs listed; otherwise `MIXED`/`UNKNOWN`. Architect confirms the set rule before phase 2.
+3. **How should unknown source gate money?** Recommend the S5-01 cautious no-amount behavior for all current money reads, including Finding Financials and consequence, with audit-only historical values still linked. Architect confirms precedence against existing `CURRENT PRICED` and LR eligibility before phase 2.

+# S4-05 — stored demonstration provenance
+
+Status: implementation-ready
+USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true — the existing Finding/Financials “Demonstration data” and “Source not confirmed” labels change source; the owner must approve mixed/unknown wording and pilot designation at the W1 checkpoint. Backend-only implementation, with no new screen, may be reviewed independently but its owner-visible label change holds merge until that checkpoint.
+Milestone: S4 Finding + Financials / S2 Evidence follow-on; size M, two phases
+Branch: `plan/stored-demo-provenance` for this packet; implement on a fresh `task/S4-05-stored-demo-provenance` branch from reviewed, merged main. Coordinator commits this packet.
+Depends on registry `A-S2-evidence`, `A-actor-identity`, `I-S2-02`, `I-finding-read`, `I-financial-read` (resolved on main). This packet does not depend on unmerged LR-01, S5-01 or S6-01.
+Planner: Codex, 2026-10-15
+
+## Outcome and authority
+
+The server records who authorized an exact demonstration publication before gateway upload, pins its tenant/Site, immutable publication ID and fixture/spec version, and binds its accepted evidence IDs. A selected FindingRevision and every consequence revision show provenance from their **actual cited evidence**, including mixed and unknown cases. A real KOBO-001 Finding on the same day as an authored fixture is never labelled demonstration by date. This closes `I-stored-demo-provenance` only after review, owner copy approval, and merge; it is required before W1 exit and any real-Site pilot, alongside the other `real_pilot_requires` gates.
+
+Authority: registry `I-stored-demo-provenance` acceptance target (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-dependency-registry-pilot.json:43`); `.agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md:17-21` Q3; `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md:12` work order item 4; actor decision `.ai/DECISIONS.md:3987-3989`; backlog `.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md:2157,2192-2199`. `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41,56` already withholds an amount for an unverified demonstration source; this packet must preserve or strengthen that gate when S5-01 later consumes the source state.
+
+## Code reality on merged main
+
+- `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:116-145,161-210` has tenant/Site-scoped `ingestion_batch`, `accepted_evidence`, `batch_id` and composite foreign keys, but no publication author or fixture version. `source_mode` is `LIVE|SIMULATED`, not proof of authorship. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:368-413` defines the ledger contract.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:264-275,287-345` authenticates a registered gateway for V2 `POST /api/ingestion/batches`; this principal only permits upload. `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/adapters/postgres_evidence_ledger.py:202-240,242-307,309-327` opens a digest-pinned batch and atomically persists accepted evidence/dispositions in chunks. It is the **existing writer to extend**; no ninth writer is proposed.
+- `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_finding_store.py:161-204,308-330` stores exact `finding_revision_evidence` IDs and reads their lineage. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/finding_read.py:350-438,446-550` exposes selected revision and Evidence reads. `backend/assetops_backend/financials/consequence_read.py:87-132,149-221` exposes consequence versions with power-interval evidence IDs and current amount status. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:247-277,284-308` serves the consequence and Financials routes.
+- The retiring heuristic is `backend/assetops_backend/demonstration_label.py:36-45,60-77`: it loads an on-disk case spec and labels solely by Site code and UTC day, or “Source not confirmed” when unreadable. `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:275-276,306` calls it on both money reads. `backend/tests/test_s4_assumption_read_api.py:269-307` asserts this old behavior; replace those assertions with stored-provenance cases.
+- `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1:621-625,678-704,828-840` rule 8 owns the ledger table allowlist and rejects other runtime database writers/driver imports. `backend/assetops_backend/publish_assumption.py:1-4,104-164` is an existing SYSTEM-only *pattern*, not a provenance publisher. `backend/TESTING.md:7-29` requires focused phase tests and one final-revision real-Postgres suite.
+
+## Implementation contract
+
+1. **Publication, before upload (new).** Add migration `0014` from main's `0013_schedule_state` head for an append-only `evidence_publication` table and a scoped immutable batch-publication binding (either a nullable `ingestion_batch.publication_id` or a one-to-one insert-only link). Publication has UUID ID; tenant/Site UUID; external batch ID, canonical Batch V2 payload SHA-256, gateway/source IDs; designation `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION`; fixture/spec ID and version; server-resolved `actor_kind=SYSTEM`, stable service principal, authorization basis, source channel, triggering job/fixture ID, and server `recorded_at`. Use composite tenant/Site FKs, unique publication identity and unique exact batch commitment; prohibit update/delete or conflicting reuse. A real/live assertion is **not** inferred from absence of designation or `source_mode=LIVE`.
+2. **Authorization (new).** Expose only a server-side administrative SYSTEM fixture/CLI command with fixed service principal and explicit configured allowlist/authorization basis, modelled on `publish_assumption.py`; no public gateway or browser write route. It must validate fixture/spec identity/version and canonical batch digest against the exact payload, then insert the publication through an extension of `PostgresEvidenceLedger`. No `--actor`, `--author` or client-supplied provenance text can become authority. Refuse wrong tenant/Site, gateway/source, missing or conflicting batch digest, publication ID reuse with changed content, and publication after any batch admission. An identical retry returns the same publication. Registration and subsequent `open_batch` must serialize for the same scoped batch key so ingestion cannot race an after-the-fact designation. The upload remains valid without designation but has `UNKNOWN` authorship.
+3. **Accepted binding (new).** `open_batch` matches only the preauthorized exact tenant/Site/gateway/source/external-batch-ID/digest commitment. The accepted evidence already references the scoped batch (`0005:165,197-200`); expose the immutable publication ID per **accepted evidence ID** through that link, including interrupted/resumed chunks. A duplicate disposition retains the original accepted ID and its original provenance; it cannot relabel old evidence. Rejected/conflict rows confer no provenance. No retroactive assignment or mutable backfill of historic accepted rows. Fail the upload if a matching publication conflicts; never silently downgrade a claimed publication to unknown.
+4. **Read and amount rules (new).** Resolve every cited accepted ID under the requested tenant/Site, returning publication ID, designation and fixture/spec version per record plus a set-level state: `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only when every relevant cited ID is bound to a consistent authored publication basis; `MIXED` when known designations differ; `UNKNOWN` for any missing/unresolved/mismatched link (and retain exact problematic IDs). A FindingRevision uses its immutable cited set, not an episode date. A consequence uses its **own** quantified power-interval evidence IDs as well as the selected FindingRevision cited set; a later revision can differ. Preserve historical revision-specific results; do not relabel a historical revision from the current head. Mixed/unknown reads say “Source not confirmed” with exact IDs and show no current amount wherever this source gate is used. Preserve `tasks/S5-01-site-overview.md:39-41` no-amount rule when its branch merges. Keep existing “Demonstration data” wording for fully authored P01; propose concise mixed/unknown copy to owner at checkpoint. Remove runtime calls to `demonstration_case` and its date/spec cache; retain its old test only as regression proving same-day real/unknown is not labelled authored.
+5. **Writer and migration boundary.** Extend the existing ledger writer's allowed tables in rule 8 (`configuration-persistence.ps1:624-625`); require INSERT only for publication/link rows, and prevent ledger or other writers from UPDATE/DELETE of these immutable rows. Preserve all eight existing writer allowlists, reverse ownership and driver non-vacuity. Add guard probes for permitted ledger INSERT, forbidden other-writer INSERT, mutation, omitted writer/import, and client route attempting designation. If LR-01 merges first and contributes migrations 0014/0015 on its branch, re-chain this migration to the new head and renumber it; never create a parallel head. Recheck S5-01 read composition after its merge without stacking this task on that unmerged branch.
+
+## Phases, proof and review
+
+| Phase | Reviewable result and focused proof |
+| --- | --- |
+| 1. Stored authorization and binding | Migration, SYSTEM publication command, ledger extension, rule-8 amendment. New focused Postgres tests: two tenants/same Site code; wrong Site, gateway/source, payload digest and post-admission refusal; client-only and gateway-credential authorship refusal; identical retry versus conflicting reuse; registration/upload race; partial batch restart; accepted/duplicate/rejected/conflict IDs; immutable SQL mutation refusal; guard probes. Review phase 1 independently. |
+| 2. Finding and consequence lineage | New scoped resolver and read DTOs; retire date heuristic in both `finding_api.py` money routes; use selected FindingRevision and consequence evidence IDs. New focused API/Postgres tests: authored P01, ordinary real/unknown reading on P01's exact day, mixed cited sets, missing publication/link, wrong-Site cited ID, historical revision, changed consequence evidence set and no-current-amount for mixed/unknown. Run adjacent Finding/Evidence/Financials tests and `tools/check-all.ps1`. Independent phase-2 review is the slice review. |
+
+At slice end run **one** final-revision real-Postgres full suite per `backend/TESTING.md`: `pytest backend/tests -n 2 --dist loadfile --no-loadscope-reorder -q --junitxml=.pytest-junit.xml`, with `ASSETOPS_TEST_DATABASE_URL` on a `*_test` database, zero failed/skipped JUnit cases, the keep-awake helper and 2× documented runtime cap. Run `node frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_dependency_registry.cjs`. Record focused/full commands, IDs, old/new API JSON, and an exact P01 plus same-day ordinary source walkthrough in the review handoff. Obtain owner wording/pilot-designation acceptance before merge because existing visible labels change. Do not claim W1 exit or pilot readiness from this task alone.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+1. **Which publication moment is authoritative?** Recommend pre-admission, digest-pinned SYSTEM registration with a serialized batch-key lock (contract above); it blocks retroactive authorship and leaves gateway transport unchanged. Architect ratifies before phase 1 if another moment is required.
+2. **Can several authored publications contribute one Finding?** Recommend `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` only if all cited IDs have the same fixture/spec version and publication basis, with exact publication IDs listed; otherwise `MIXED`/`UNKNOWN`. Architect confirms the set rule before phase 2.
+3. **How should unknown source gate money?** Recommend the S5-01 cautious no-amount behavior for all current money reads, including Finding Financials and consequence, with audit-only historical values still linked. Architect confirms precedence against existing `CURRENT PRICED` and LR eligibility before phase 2.

apply patch
patch: completed
